# MapOS v19 — úplná delta traceability (322/322)

> Evidence date: 2026-09-02. This file preserves every source ID and original acceptance. A
> source file or test file by itself is never treated as verification: verified rows point to
> passing run evidence. `verified-offline` makes no VPS/provider/public-registry claim.

Source: attached archive member `mapos-source-grounded-master-plan-2026-09-01/04_REQUIREMENTS_TRACEABILITY_SOURCE_GROUNDED.md` (SHA-256 `1372e028bf44ff57998f60220d20e4e32555630cc15be16427924bd45b8b4295`). The archive was not modified.

## Validation and counts

Validation: **322 rows / 322 unique IDs / 0 missing / 0 unexpected**.

| v19 state                | Count |
| ------------------------ | ----: |
| `verified-runtime`       |    73 |
| `verified-offline`       |   181 |
| `accepted-external-gate` |    14 |
| `superseded-with-adr`    |     7 |
| `open`                   |    47 |

Operational delta roll-up (secondary classification; the canonical state remains the limited vocabulary above):

| Delta class | Count | Meaning                                                            |
| ----------- | ----: | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| VERIFIED    |   254 | runtime/offline acceptance has passing evidence                    |
| PARTIAL     |    33 | `open`; a useful boundary exists but full acceptance is not proved |
| BLOCKED     |    14 | accepted external provider/infrastructure gate                     |
| GAP         |    14 | `open`; no runnable v19 path covers the acceptance                 |
| SUPERSEDED  |     7 | safer ADR disposition replaces the literal requirement             |

## Evidence policy

- `verified-runtime` is used only where a scoped live record exists: the pre-refactor baseline (`QUAL-001`), the r5 public Discover AI response (`DISC-021`), r6 durable plan collaboration (`PLAN-024`), r7 live route alternatives (`PLAN-027`), r8 deterministic adventure routing (`PLAN-025`), r10 real dated weather context (`PLAN-006`), the eight r11 AppShell layout acceptances (`LAY-007`, `LAY-009`, `LAY-015`, `LAY-016`, `LAY-018`, `LAY-019`, `LAY-020`, `LAY-022`), the fourteen r12 Layers/Basemaps/footer acceptances (`LAYER-002`, `LAYER-005`, `LAYER-006`, `LAYER-009`, `LAYER-010`, `LAYER-012`, `LAYER-013`, `LAYER-014`, `LAYER-015`, `LAYER-023`, `BASE-002`, `BASE-003`, `BASE-006`, `FOOT-006`), the eight r13 Search/basemap-recovery acceptances (`BASE-015`, `BASE-016`, `SEARCH-002`, `SEARCH-004`, `SEARCH-005`, `SEARCH-006`, `SEARCH-007`, `SEARCH-010`), the four r14 Settings acceptances (`SET-001`, `SET-002`, `SET-003`, `SET-004`), the nine r15 Personal acceptances (`PER-004`, `PER-006`, `PER-007`, `PER-009`, `PER-010`, `PER-012`, `PER-013`, `PER-014`, `PER-020`) and the five r16 global-task acceptances (`LOAD-002`, `LOAD-003`, `LOAD-004`, `LOAD-005`, `LOAD-007`).
- The r17 live weather record adds `WX-010`: each seven-day row opens its own available three-hour detail without horizontal overflow.
- The r18 live Events record adds `EVT-006` through `EVT-010`: one nonlinear annual timeline and a responsive Discover explorer synchronize time/type/price/distance filters, map/list/count and sourced detail actions without mislabelling unknown price.
- The r19 live Discover record adds `DISC-002` and a scoped real-boundary slice for `DISC-001`, `DISC-003` and `DISC-004`: the cached provider returns a zoom-selected polygon, the map keeps it after panel close and tap reopens the named context.
- The r20 live Discover record adds `DISC-016` and `DISC-022`: explicit refresh has one visible cancellable TaskRegistry entry, while the four visible presets drive editable layers, local recommendations and the server context use case.
- The r21 live Discover record adds `DISC-018` and `DISC-019`: generic source-aware cards expose dated regional population and GDP/person with scope and uncertainty. `DISC-020` moves from gap to partial because both providers use the same UI collection, while a separate capability registry remains open.
- The r22 live Discover record adds `DISC-001`, `DISC-003`, `DISC-004`, `DISC-007`, `DISC-008` and `MAP-017`: a viewport-bounded Europe-wide GISCO catalogue renders one NUTS level, direct candidate selection refreshes context, locality zoom removes mismatched regional candidates and POI hit-testing stays above the overlay.
- The r23 live weather record adds `WX-005`, `WX-013` and `WX-015`: the regional colour field has a matching legend, touch opens the same exact cell detail as hover, and variable/zoom changes preserve one MapLibre instance, source and base style.
- `verified-offline` requires a passing local automated run recorded in the v19 run artifact or a scoped phase evidence record.
- Browser test source that was not freshly executed is useful partial evidence only and cannot create a verified state by itself.
- `QUAL-004`, `QUAL-011` and `QUAL-017` are explicitly `pending-live` until v19 VPS evidence is attached.
- Fixture/provider-neutral cores never stand in for a real payment provider, actual model payload, official indexer or public package release.

| `GATE-LIVE-V19` — V19 VPS deployment verification                            | `QUAL-004`, `QUAL-011`, `QUAL-017`                                             | `open`                   | Deploy v19, record candidate/live PostGIS EXPLAIN, data-rights PostgreSQL drill, health/security evidence, backup/restore and rollback identity. [scripts/deploy-vps.sh](../scripts/deploy-vps.sh), [scripts/smoke-vps-public.sh](../scripts/smoke-vps-public.sh)                                                                                                                       |
